          <p>Any amounts paid by an insurer pursuant to a judgment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.444">115B.444</a> or settlement under this section reduce the outstanding coverage available under policies of the insurer to the extent permitted under applicable law and policy provisions.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.443.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/370/#laws.0.3.0">1996 c 370 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.444">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.444 STATE ACTION AGAINST INSURERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.444.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">State action.</span></h2>
          <p>The state, by the attorney general, may bring a state action against any insurer for recovery of all environmental response costs incurred and to be incurred by the state, which costs are related to qualified facilities for which the state has assumed response action obligations or responsibilities under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.39">115B.39</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.43">115B.43</a>, and for which costs policyholders of the insurer may be liable. No assignment of any rights of a policyholder to the state and no judgment against the policyholder is required as a condition for the state bringing an action under this subdivision. The state shall make reasonable efforts to notify affected policyholders of the state's commencement of an action under this section. An affected policyholder may intervene in an action under this section. For purposes of this section, an "affected policyholder" means a policyholder whose rights under an insurance policy relevant to an action under this section may be affected by the action. All defenses available to a policyholder to any claim of liability for environmental response costs asserted or which could be asserted against it shall be available to the insurer in an action brought by the state under this subdivision. In any action under this subdivision, the claim of the state shall be limited by the applicable terms, conditions, and provisions of the relevant insurance policy under which coverage may be provided, and the state shall have no greater rights than the rights of the policyholder under its insurance policy subject to the statutory and common law that applies to the determination of those rights. Nothing in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.441">115B.441</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.445">115B.445</a> shall be construed to relieve any policyholder of liability for environmental response costs to the extent of any insurance coverage of the policyholder by reason of the assumption of obligations or responsibilities by the state for environmental response actions under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.39">115B.39</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.43">115B.43</a>. Before the attorney general may commence an action against an insurer under this subdivision, for any claims with respect to a qualified facility, the attorney general and the commissioner shall present to the insurer a written settlement offer, and shall provide the insurer with an opportunity to negotiate and enter a settlement with the state as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.443">115B.443</a>. In any action under this subdivision, the state shall have the same rights as individual policyholders to recover its reasonable expenses and costs of litigation, including attorney fees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.444.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Actions by policyholders; state approval of settlements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), nothing in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.441">115B.441</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.444">115B.444</a> affects the right of a policyholder to bring or pursue any action against, or enter any settlement with, an insurer for any claims for which the state has a right of action against the insurer under this section and that have not been resolved by a settlement or judgment under this section. The state may intervene in an action in which a policyholder seeks to recover a claim for which the state has a right of action under this section.</p>
          <p>(b) A policyholder may not enter a settlement that releases an insurer from any claims for which the state has an action under subdivision 1, unless the attorney general has given prior written approval to the settlement and the policyholder agrees to assign to the state any amounts recovered under the settlement from the insurer that are attributable to the resolution of the claims.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.444.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/370/#laws.0.4.0">1996 c 370 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.445">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.445 DEPOSITING PROCEEDS.</h1>
        <p>All amounts paid to the state by an insurer pursuant to any settlement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.443">115B.443</a> or judgment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.444">115B.444</a> must be deposited in the state treasury and credited equally to the remediation fund and the closed landfill investment fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.445.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/370/#laws.0.5.0">1996 c 370 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.2.31.0">2003 c 128 art 2 s 31</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.115B.45"><b>115B.45</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/370/#laws.0.7.0">1996 c 370 s 7</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.115B.46"><b>115B.46</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/370/#laws.0.7.0">1996 c 370 s 7</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DRY CLEANER ENVIRONMENTAL RESPONSE AND REIMBURSEMENT LAW</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.47">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.47 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.47">115B.47</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.51">115B.51</a> may be cited as the "Dry Cleaner Environmental Response and Reimbursement Law."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.47.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/252/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 252 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.48">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.48 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.02">115B.02</a> and this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.47">115B.47</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.51">115B.51</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Dry cleaner environmental response and reimbursement account; account.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dry cleaner environmental response and reimbursement account" or "account" means the dry cleaner environmental response and reimbursement account in the remediation fund established in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49">115B.49</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.155">116.155</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Dry cleaning facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dry cleaning facility" means a facility located in this state that is or has been used for a dry cleaning operation, other than:</p>
          <p>(1) a coin-operated dry cleaning operation;</p>
          <p>(2) a facility located on a United States military base;</p>
          <p>(3) a uniform service or linen supply facility;</p>
          <p>(4) a prison or other penal institution;</p>
          <p>(5) a facility on the national priorities list established under the federal Superfund Act; or</p>
          <p>(6) a facility at which a response action has been taken or started before July 1, 1995, except as authorized in a settlement agreement approved by the commissioner by July 1, 1997.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Dry cleaning operation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dry cleaning operation" means commercial cleaning of apparel and household fabrics for the general public, using one or more dry cleaning solvents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Dry cleaning solvent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dry cleaning solvent" means any nonaqueous solvent for use in the cleaning of garments or other fabrics at a dry cleaning facility, including, but not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) perchloroethylene and its degradation products; and</p>
          <p>(2) petroleum-based solvents and their degradation products.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Environmental response costs.</span></h2>
          <p>"Environmental response costs" means those costs described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.6">115B.17, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Facility" means one or more buildings or parts of a building and the equipment, installations, and structures contained in the building, located on a single site or on contiguous or adjacent sites. Facility includes any site or area where a hazardous substance, or a pollutant or contaminant, has been deposited, stored, disposed of, or placed, or otherwise comes to be located.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Full-time equivalence.</span></h2>
          <p>"Full-time equivalence" means 2,000 hours worked by employees, owners, and others in a dry cleaning facility during a 12-month period beginning July 1 of the preceding year and running through June 30 of the year in which the annual registration fee is due. For those dry cleaning facilities that were in business less than the 12-month period, full-time equivalence means the total of all of the hours worked in the dry cleaning facility, divided by 2,000 and multiplied by a fraction, the numerator of which is 50 and the denominator of which is the number of weeks in business during the reporting period. For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49">115B.49</a>, an owner working 2,000 hours or more shall be considered as one full-time equivalent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/123/#laws.0.6.0">2016 c 123 s 6</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.48.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Owner or operator.</span></h2>
          <p>"Owner or operator" means a person who:</p>
          <p>(1) owns or has owned a dry cleaning facility during the time the dry cleaning facility operated; or</p>
          <p>(2) operates or has operated a dry cleaning facility.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/252/#laws.0.2.0">1995 c 252 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/471/#laws.2.1.0">1996 c 471 art 2 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.110.0">1997 c 216 s 110</a>,111; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/324/#laws.0.1.0">2002 c 324 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.2.32.0">2003 c 128 art 2 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.133.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 133</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.5.2.0">2006 c 281 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.4.113.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 4 s 113</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/123/#laws.0.1.0">2016 c 123 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.49">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.49 DRY CLEANER ENVIRONMENTAL RESPONSE AND REIMBURSEMENT ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The dry cleaner environmental response and reimbursement account is established as an account in the remediation fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Revenue sources.</span></h2>
          <p>Revenue from the following sources must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to the account:</p>
          <p>(1) the proceeds of the fees imposed by subdivision 4;</p>
          <p>(2) interest attributable to investment of money in the account;</p>
          <p>(3) penalties and interest collected under subdivision 4, paragraph (c); and</p>
          <p>(4) money received by the commissioner for deposit in the account in the form of gifts, grants, and appropriations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Expenditures.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Money in the account may be used:</p>
          <p>(1) for environmental response costs incurred by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.50#stat.115B.50.1">115B.50, subdivision 1</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) for reimbursement of amounts spent by the commissioner from the remediation fund for expenses described in clause (1);</p>
          <p>(3) for reimbursements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.50#stat.115B.50.2">115B.50, subdivision 2</a>; and</p>
          <p>(4) for administrative costs of the commissioner of revenue.</p>
          <p>(b) Money in the account is appropriated to the commissioner for the purposes of this subdivision. The commissioner shall transfer funds to the commissioner of revenue sufficient to cover administrative costs pursuant to paragraph (a), clause (4).</p>
          <p>(c) In making reimbursements from the account, the commissioner must give priority to the following cases, in descending order:</p>
          <p>(1) reimbursements for amounts spent on response actions by a person who meets the conditions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.48#stat.115B.48.10">115B.48, subdivision 10</a>, clause (2); and</p>
          <p>(2) reimbursements for expenditures made by the commissioner under paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2), on behalf of owners or operators when the owner or operator failed to complete the requested response action and the commissioner undertook the response action to remediate the property.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Registration; fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The owner or operator of a dry cleaning facility shall register on or before October 1 of each year with the commissioner of revenue in a manner prescribed by the commissioner of revenue and pay a registration fee for the facility. The amount of the fee is:</p>
          <p>(1) $500, for facilities with a full-time equivalence of fewer than five;</p>
          <p>(2) $1,000, for facilities with a full-time equivalence of five to ten; and</p>
          <p>(3) $1,500, for facilities with a full-time equivalence of more than ten.</p>
          <p class="bl">The registration fee must be paid on or before October 18 or the owner or operator of a dry cleaning facility may elect to pay the fee in equal installments. Installment payments must be paid on or before October 18, on or before January 18, on or before April 18, and on or before June 18. All payments made after October 18 bear interest at the rate specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.40">270C.40</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A person who sells dry cleaning solvents for use by dry cleaning facilities in the state shall collect and remit to the commissioner of revenue in the same manner prescribed by the commissioner of revenue, for the taxes imposed under chapter 297A, a fee of:</p>
          <p>(1) $3.50 for each gallon of perchloroethylene sold for use by dry cleaning facilities in the state;</p>
          <p>(2) 70 cents for each gallon of hydrocarbon-based dry cleaning solvent sold for use by dry cleaning facilities in the state; and</p>
          <p>(3) 35 cents for each gallon of other nonaqueous solvents sold for use by dry cleaning facilities in the state.</p>
          <p>(c) The audit, assessment, appeal, collection, enforcement, and administrative provisions of chapters 270C and 289A apply to the fee imposed by this subdivision. To enforce this subdivision, the commissioner of revenue may grant extensions to file returns and pay fees, impose penalties and interest on the annual registration fee under paragraph (a) and the monthly fee under paragraph (b), and abate penalties and interest in the manner provided in chapters 270C and 289A. The penalties and interest imposed on taxes under chapter 297A apply to the fees imposed under this subdivision. Disclosure of data collected by the commissioner of revenue under this subdivision is governed by chapter 270B.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/10/#laws.1.82.0">2005 c 10 art 1 s 82</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/157/#laws.0.2.0">2005 c 157 s 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.49.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Fee adjustment.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1285">16A.1285</a>, each fiscal year the commissioner shall adjust the fees in subdivision 4 as necessary to maintain an annual income to the account of $650,000.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.49.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/252/#laws.0.3.0">1995 c 252 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/471/#laws.2.3.0">1996 c 471 art 2 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.112.0">1997 c 216 s 112</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/250/#laws.3.14.0">1999 c 250 art 3 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/333/#laws.0.1.0">2000 c 333 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.128.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 128</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/324/#laws.0.2.0">2002 c 324 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.2.33.0">2003 c 128 art 2 s 33</a>,34; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.2.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/157/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 157 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.7.1.0">2006 c 259 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.11.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/5/#laws.0.1.0">2021 c 5 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.491">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.491 DRY CLEANING FACILITY USE FEE; FACILITIES TO FILE RETURN.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.491.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Use fee.</span></h2>
          <p>A dry cleaning facility that purchases dry cleaning solvents for use in Minnesota without paying the seller of dry cleaning solvents the fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49#stat.115B.49.4">115B.49, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b), is subject to an equivalent fee. Liability for the fee is incurred when dry cleaning solvents are received in Minnesota by the dry cleaning facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.491.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Return required.</span></h2>
          <p>On or before the 20th of each calendar month, every dry cleaning facility that has purchased dry cleaning solvents for use in this state during the preceding calendar month, upon which the fee imposed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49#stat.115B.49.4">115B.49, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b), has not been paid to the seller of the dry cleaning solvents, shall file a return with the commissioner of revenue showing the quantity of solvents purchased and a computation of the fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49#stat.115B.49.4">115B.49, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (c). The fee must accompany the return. The return must be made upon a form furnished and prescribed by the commissioner of revenue and must contain such other information as the commissioner of revenue may require.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.491.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>All of the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.49#stat.115B.49.4">115B.49, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (c), apply to this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.491.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/471/#laws.2.5.0">1996 c 471 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/250/#laws.3.16.0">1999 c 250 art 3 s 16</a>,17</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.492">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.492 ALLOCATING PAYMENT.</h1>
        <p>In the discretion of the commissioner of revenue, payments received for fees may be credited first to the oldest liability not secured by a judgment or lien. For liabilities to which payments are applied, the commissioner of revenue may credit payments first to penalties, next to interest, and then to the fee due.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.492.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/471/#laws.2.6.0">1996 c 471 art 2 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.50">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.50 RESPONSE ACTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.50.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Response actions by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In accordance with the priority list established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.13">115B.17, subdivision 13</a>, the commissioner shall take all response actions at or related to a dry cleaning facility that the commissioner determines are reasonable and necessary to protect the public health or welfare or the environment under the standards required in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.01">115B.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.20">115B.20</a>. The commissioner shall review and approve any investigation and response action plan submitted by an owner or operator of a dry cleaning facility who is taking response actions under subdivision 2 and shall oversee the implementation of the approved plans. In carrying out the duties under this subdivision, the commissioner may take emergency removal actions as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.1">115B.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), and may exercise the authority provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17">115B.17</a>, subdivisions 2 to 5, 15, and 16.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may not seek recovery against a current or former owner or operator of a dry cleaning facility of any environmental response costs in excess of $10,000 incurred by the commissioner at the facility, except:</p>
          <p>(1) to the extent of insurance coverage, in excess of $10,000, held by the owner or operator; or</p>
          <p>(2) as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.51">115B.51</a>.</p>
          <p class="bl">If the commissioner seeks recovery of environmental response costs against an owner or operator pursuant to this paragraph, the owner or operator shall act as directed by the commissioner to assert any rights of the owner or operator to any insurance coverage applicable to those costs and, if coverage is denied, to assign those rights to the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) Before taking a response action under this subdivision, the commissioner shall notify the owner or operator of the facility. Except for emergency removal actions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.1">115B.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), the commissioner shall not take response actions under this subdivision at a dry cleaning facility where an owner or operator of the facility is taking response actions under subdivision 2 in accordance with an investigation or response action plan approved by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.50.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Response actions by owners or operators; reimbursement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At the request of the owner or operator of a dry cleaning facility who takes response actions at the facility in accordance with a response action plan approved by the commissioner, the commissioner shall reimburse the owner or operator for all but $10,000 of the environmental response costs incurred by the owner or operator if the commissioner determines that the costs are reasonable and were actually incurred. If a request for reimbursement is denied, the owner or operator may appeal the decision as a contested case under chapter 14.</p>
          <p>(b) If the commissioner reimburses an owner or operator for environmental response costs under this subdivision for which the owner or operator has insurance coverage, the commissioner is subrogated to the rights of the owner or operator with respect to that insurance coverage to the extent of the reimbursement. Acceptance of reimbursement under this subdivision constitutes an assignment by the owner or operator with respect to any insurance coverage applicable to the costs that are reimbursed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.50.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Expenditures limited.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may not, in a single fiscal year, make expenditures from the account related to a single dry cleaning facility that exceed $100,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.50.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reimbursement adjustment; rulemaking.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may use the expedited rulemaking process under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.389">14.389</a> to adjust reimbursement dollar amounts contained in the rules established under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/252/#laws.0.4.0">1995 c 252 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.73.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/123/#laws.0.2.0">2016 c 123 s 2</a>,3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.51">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.51 ILLEGAL ACTIONS.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner may recover under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.6">115B.17, subdivision 6</a>, that portion of the environmental response costs at a dry cleaning facility that is attributable to a person who otherwise would be responsible for the release or threatened release under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.03">115B.03</a>, and whose actions related to the release or threatened release were in violation of federal or state hazardous waste management laws in effect at the time of those actions.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.51.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/252/#laws.0.5.0">1995 c 252 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.52">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.52 WATER QUALITY AND SUSTAINABILITY ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.52.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.53">115B.53</a>, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "East metropolitan area" includes but is not limited to the cities of Woodbury, Oakdale, Lake Elmo, Cottage Grove, St. Paul Park, Afton, and Newport and the townships of West Lakeland and Grey Cloud Island.</p>
          <p>(c) "Settlement" means the agreement and order entered on February 20, 2018, settling litigation commenced by the state against the 3M Company under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17">115B.17</a>, subdivision 7.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.52.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The water quality and sustainability account is established as an account in the remediation fund. The account consists of revenue deposited in the account under the terms of the settlement and earnings on the investment of money in the account. Money in the account may be invested through the State Board of Investment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.52.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Expenditures.</span></h2>
          <p>Money in the account is appropriated to the commissioner of the Pollution Control Agency and to the commissioner of natural resources for the purposes authorized under the settlement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.52.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of the Pollution Control Agency and the commissioner of natural resources must jointly submit:</p>
          <p>(1) by April 1, 2019, an implementation plan detailing how the commissioners will:</p>
          <p>(i) determine how the priorities in the settlement will be met and how the spending will move from the first priority to the second priority and the second priority to the third priority outlined in the settlement; and</p>
          <p>(ii) evaluate and determine what projects receive funding;</p>
          <p>(2) by October 1 each year, a report to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative policy and finance committees with jurisdiction over environment and natural resources on expenditures from the water quality and sustainability account during the previous fiscal year; and</p>
          <p>(3) by October 1, 2023, and each year thereafter, a report to the legislature on expenditures from the water quality and sustainability account during the previous fiscal year and a spending plan for anticipated expenditures from the account during the current fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115B.52.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Local approval.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of the Pollution Control Agency or commissioner of natural resources must receive approval from the local unit of government prior to assuming control or otherwise operating an existing municipal water supply operation in the east metropolitan area.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.52.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/204/#laws.0.1.0">2018 c 204 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.3.19.0">2023 c 60 art 3 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115B.53">
        <h1 class="shn">115B.53 WATER QUALITY AND SUSTAINABILITY STAKEHOLDERS.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of the Pollution Control Agency and the commissioner of natural resources must work with stakeholders to identify and recommend projects to receive funding from the water quality and sustainability account under the settlement. Stakeholders include, at a minimum, representatives of the agency, the Department of Natural Resources, east metropolitan area municipalities, and the 3M Company. The commissioners must establish a process to solicit and evaluate the recommendations from municipalities in the east metropolitan area as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.52">115B.52</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115B.53.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/204/#laws.0.2.0">2018 c 204 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
